Object with concrete repros; do not fix.\n\nRead `git show b7a573e` first to see exactly what changed, then read the current: sigil/agents/actor.py, actor_gate.py, actor_scope.py, web_engine.py, vault.py, sources.py, and tests/test_actor.py.\n\nThe fixes to verify (re-run the exploit, prove it is now blocked):\n1. execute() single-shot: one owner approval must authorise EXACTLY ONE engine.act. Write a repro (scratchpad, ~/.sigil/venv/bin/python) that approves one purchase step and calls execute(seq) 5 times → assert exactly ONE act. THEN try to DEFEAT the single-shot: (a) approve the SAME step twice (two approval records, same target_seq) then execute twice — does the guard still hold? (b) Re-PREVIEW the identical action to mint a NEW seq (new queued record, same token/journal) and approve+execute that — is that a per-action re-authorization (acceptable, owner signed again) or an unintended cap/replay bypass? Reason about whether the applied-record scan keys correctly on target_seq and whether the guard can be starved (e.g. an applied record written under a different signal/status).\n2. creation cap at execute: batch-preview 3 account.create (cap=1), approve all 3, execute all 3 → assert exactly ONE act and exactly ONE applied account.create. THEN try to defeat it: interleave preview/execute; use 3 DISTINCT services vs the SAME service; check whether a queued (awaiting-approval) or refused record is miscounted by creation_allowed (it counts status=='applied' only). Is there any ordering where 2 slip through?\n3. url bound into action_token + journal keyed by token: two same-origin URLs (/login,/promo) with identical HTML → must get DIFFERENT tokens and DIFFERENT journals, and executing the approved /login must POST to /login. Re-run the red-pen BLOCK-3 repro and confirm it now fails to rebind. THEN probe: can two DIFFERENT (service,kind,url,field_binding) tuples still collide to one journal file? Can a journal-URL edit still slip past (execute re-derives the token from the journaled url + fetched page — confirm an edited journal url makes fresh != token → abort)? Does the origin_allowed re-check at execute actually run before the fetch/act?\n4. pinned-IP threading (anti-rebind): confirm actor.execute passes pv.resolved_ip into engine.act, and HttpEngine.act uses pinned_ip when supplied (binding the POST to the fetch's vetted IP). Check the FALLBACK: when pinned_ip is None/\"\" HttpEngine re-vets via _vetted_ip — is there a path where the actor calls act with a MISSING ip on a real (non-fake) fetch such that the rebind gap reopens? (Trace: on a successful fetch HttpEngine returns resolved_ip=ip; on a non-ok fetch the actor aborts at block/token before act. So act is only reached with a real ip — verify that's airtight.) Also confirm the SHARED sources.fetch_raw change (added resolved_ip) did not break SCRIBE — run tests/test_scrape.py.\n5. field-source validation: unknown sources (env:/file:/vault:unknownkey) and non-string values must be REFUSED at preview (not silently dropped, not crash). Confirm; then check `literal:` edge cases (a literal that is itself `literal:vault:password`, an empty literal, a literal containing the field separators `;`/`|`/`=` used in field_binding — could a crafted literal value forge a field_binding that changes the token meaning or collides with a vault-ref encoding? Trace _field_binding + _redact_source: literals are reduced to a 16-char content hash, so raw literal bytes never enter the binding — verify a literal cannot spoof `vaultvN|...` or a `name=vault:password` entry).\n6. honesty: confirm web_engine.py no longer references BrowserEngine/needs_js/headless fallback, and no other module imports web_engine.needs_js (grep). Confirm the actor is still HTTP-only (exactly one engine.act call site, no browser/subprocess/escalate path).\n7. credential-leak re-scan on the FIXED code: with the new applied-record target_seq field and the new refusal records, re-scan the whole spine bytes for a unique password sentinel after a full preview→approve→execute → assert absent. Confirm the new refusal/finding payloads (origin-recheck, cap-at-execute) carry NO field values.\n\nFinally: run the FULL suite (`for t in tests/test_*.py; do ~/.sigil/venv/bin/python \"$t\" | tail -1; done`) and the Rust tests (`cd kernel && cargo test --release 2>&1 | grep \"test result\"`) and report the totals. \n\nReturn: for each of 1–7, StATUS = HELD (with the repro output showing the exploit is dead) or BROKEN (with a concrete new repro). Plus any NEW defect the fixes introduced, ranked by severity. If everything holds, give an explicit attestation naming what you re-ran and why it is now sound. Be adversarial and concrete — a working bypass is worth far more than a hypothesis."},"uuid":"9cd0611f-050c-4007-a091-590e6fe2ef60","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:28:42.440Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"9cd0611f-050c-4007-a091-590e6fe2ef60","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"5ef833af-9b6b-4bc3-a5b8-ed99e00a9fe6","agentId":"a9f88e85e8b6db51d","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"<command-message>crucible</command-message>\n<command-name>crucible</command-name>\n<skill-format>true</skill-format>"},{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main/.claude/skills/crucible\n\n# CRUCIBLE — operating the engine\n\nCRUCIBLE turns an authorized seed URL into **oracle-confirmed** findings, reasons over them,\nand produces auditable reports — inside a fail-closed safety stack. Its whole value is one\nrule; internalize it before anything else:\n\n> **A claim is a FACT only when a deterministic oracle fires over data a real target\n> produced.** You (the LLM) ADVISE where to look and what a result might mean; the oracle\n> CONFIRMS. Nothing else — not your confidence, not a critic's endorsement, not a plausible\n> story — promotes a claim to a fact.\n\nThe standing doctrine every reasoning call operates under is\n[framework/cognitive/metacognition.md](../../../framework/cognitive/metacognition.md) (it is\ninjected into every LLM system prompt). The full architecture — agents, event spine, veracity\nlayer, learning/metacognition core, safety stack — is\n[framework/v2/docs/CRUCIBLE-AI.md](../../../framework/v2/docs/CRUCIBLE-AI.md). The binding\nconstitution is [CLAUDE.md](../../../CLAUDE.md); never relax scope, destruction, evidence, or\nhonesty rules.\n\n## The invariants (never violate these)\n\n1. **Oracle authority.** Only a fired oracle confirms. Critics/RL/reflection/self-consistency\n   may only *advise, re-rank, defer, or abstain* — never promote a finding or override the oracle.\n2. **Coverage.** Never silently skip an authorized attack surface. Learning and reflection\n   *deprioritise* (re-rank / defer); they never gate a surface out.\n3. **Prove by re-execution.** A finding is a fact only if its retained `oracle_context`\n   re-fires. The veracity firewall (`framework/v2/veracity/`) re-runs proofs; it can only demote.\n4. **Non-circular learning.** An EXPLOITABLE label needs ≥2 independent corroborating oracle\n   kinds; a silent oracle is never auto-labelled a false positive.\n5. **Refuse honestly, fail closed.** Decline to conclude what you cannot ground; record every\n   refusal as evidence. Hard limits (scope, authorization, destruction, real user data) are\n   inviolable — when unsure, stop and ask.\n6. **Determinism + append-only.** No wallclock/global-rng in learning/reward math; the event\n   spine is append-only (supersede, never edit).\n\n## Structured workflows\n\n### Run an authorized engagement (end-to-end, gated)\n1. Confirm the active target's `targets/<slug>/charter.md` authorizes it (constitution §II).\n2. `python3 -m framework.v2 engage <slug> <seed-url> [--recon] [--spine]`\n   — crawls, audits, confirms via oracles, chains attack paths, scores confidence, and\n   (with `--spine`) mirrors the whole run onto the immutable blackboard event stream.\n   (`--strict-evidence` is a **`scan`**-only flag, below — not an `engage` flag.)\n3. Every request passes the full gate chain; an out-of-scope seed or tripped kill-switch\n   refuses *before* traffic and is recorded as a `refusal` event.\n\n### Loopback-only quick scan\n`python3 -m framework.v2 scan http://127.0.0.1:<port>/ --format json|sarif|html [--strict-evidence]`\n— the export states each finding's **live grounding** (fact / ungrounded / contradicted);\n`--strict-evidence` withholds non-fact findings from the rendered doc but keeps them in\n`--reverifiable-out`.\n\n### Re-verify a report (prove-don't-guess, offline)\n`python3 -m framework.v2 verify <report.json>` re-runs each finding's retained `oracle_context`.\n`python3 -m framework.v2 evidence ...` builds/verifies signed, hash-linked evidence certificates\n(and, with report claims bound in, fails closed when a report sentence has no backing evidence).\n\n### Inspect / audit the event spine\nThe blackboard (`framework/v2/agents/blackboard.py`) is the one append-only, typed, provenance-\nlinked event stream. Replay it with `Blackboard.replay(engagement=..., since_id=...)`.\nCryptographic tamper-evidence: `framework/v2/agents/spine_chain.py`\n(`build_spine_chain` / `sign_spine_head` / `verify_spine_head`).\n\n### Recon / intel, memory, self-improvement\n`python3 -m framework.v2 intel ...` (OSINT into the shared world-model; gated egress),\n`memory ...` (cross-engagement priors — never fabricate a score), `improve ...`\n(authorise-not-apply — it never self-applies a change).\n\n### Read-only operator console\n`python3 -m framework.v2 console` — loopback, read-only, zero-impact operator UI over the run.\n\n## What NOT to do\n- Do not build detection-evasion / identity-rotation / stay-hidden-from-defenders capability —\n  CRUCIBLE is correlatable and authorized by doctrine (constitution §VI).\n- Do not let any learned/critic/LLM signal enter the deterministic oracle/SCE/calibration inputs\n  or promote a finding.\n- Do not attack third parties (payment/IdP/CDN) — test only the operator's integration with them.\n"}]},"isMeta":true,"uuid":"06cabe86-1e12-47db-b69a-9e04b293262b","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:28:42.451Z","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"c208f2b7-7484-46be-9107-f4a69eaba7b8","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"5ef833af-9b6b-4bc3-a5b8-ed99e00a9fe6","agentId":"a9f88e85e8b6db51d","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01HRCuKuBTFXckZtRZw1rDRj","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"DELEGATE (Phase 8, WS-G) — the owner-consented identity/account manager + web actor. Manages the\n2\towner's OWN credentials and, with PER-ACTION owner approval, creates accounts / logs in / fills forms /\n3\tsubmits on the owner's behalf. Ceiling A2, and added to NO_PROMOTION_AGENTS — every account/login/\n4\tsubmit/purchase is A3 (explicit, per-action, owner-signed, no promotion; the WARDEN oracle forces this\n5\tfrom the honest tool name). Fuses the Operator transaction (plan → preview-by-reference →\n6\ttoken-bound-approve → re-derive-at-execute → verify) with the egress per-action token.\n7\t\n8\tOffense-free by construction:\n9\t  • NO `as_identity`/impersonate parameter exists — fields resolve ONLY from the owner's OWN vault.\n10\t  • a DETECTED block (best-effort: CAPTCHA / 403 / 429) STOPS and is surfaced as a positive control; the\n11\t    block-check precedes any action, and there is NO browser-escalation code path in the actor at all\n12\t    (HTTP-only), so \"use a browser to beat a block\" is structurally unreachable.\n13\t  • ONE approval authorises exactly ONE action: execute is single-shot per step (a second execute of an\n14\t    already-applied step is refused), so an approval can never be replayed into repeated POSTs.\n15\t  • per-service creation cap (mass creation is out of doctrine), enforced at preview AND re-checked at\n16\t    execute (so a batch previewed before any execute cannot outrun the cap); `ActorScope` origin\n17\t    allowlist (deny-all), re-checked at execute.\n18\t  • credentials resolve from the keyring ONLY at the last instant of execute, into a local var — never a\n19\t    Proposal payload, never logged/journaled. The executable step (incl. any literal values) lives in a\n20\t    0700 journal OFF the append-only spine; the spine binds by service + URL + page hash + a field_binding\n21\t    of NAMES + vault-references + literal-content-hashes + the vault VERSION (a changed URL OR page OR a\n22\t    credential rotation OR a literal edit changes the token → the approval no longer verifies).\"\"\"\n23\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n24\t\n25\timport json\n26\timport os\n27\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\n28\tfrom pathlib import Path\n29\tfrom typing import List\n30\t\n31\tfrom ..config import SIGIL_HOME\n32\tfrom ..reuse import sha256_hex\n33\tfrom .actor_gate import ACTION_SIGNAL, action_approved, action_token\n34\tfrom .actor_scope import ActorScope\n35\tfrom .base import Agent, AgentResult, Proposal, Tier\n36\tfrom .web_engine import HttpEngine, detect_block\n37\t\n38\t_ACTOR_HOME = SIGIL_HOME / \"actor\"\n39\t_JOURNAL = _ACTOR_HOME / \"steps\"     # executable steps (WITH any literal values) — off the append-only spine\n40\t\n41\t# honest tool names — the WARDEN oracle forces each to A3 (explicit, per-action, no-promotion)\n42\t_TOOL = {\"navigate\": \"browser.navigate\", \"fill\": \"form.fill\", \"submit\": \"submit.form\",\n43\t         \"account.create\": \"account.create\", \"login\": \"login\", \"purchase\": \"purchase.item\"}\n44\t\n45\t_VAULT_REFS = {\"vault:password\", \"vault:email\", \"vault:username\"}\n46\t\n47\t\n48\tdef _valid_source(src) -> bool:\n49\t    \"\"\"A field value is a known source: an owner-vault reference or a literal. Anything else (unknown\n50\t    vault key, `env:`/`file:` scheme, non-string) is REFUSED at preview — so the owner never approves a\n51\t    field the actor would silently drop, and no unhandled source can reach execute.\"\"\"\n52\t    return isinstance(src, str) and (src in _VAULT_REFS or src.startswith(\"literal:\"))\n53\t\n54\t\n55\tdef _secure(d: Path) -> None:\n56\t    d.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n57\t    try:\n58\t        os.chmod(d, 0o700)\n59\t    except OSError:\n60\t        pass\n61\t\n62\t\n63\tdef _redact_source(src: str) -> str:\n64\t    \"\"\"Spine-safe rendering of a field source: vault references pass through (they are references, not\n65\t    values); a literal is reduced to a content hash (the value is journaled 0700, never on the spine).\"\"\"\n66\t    if src.startswith(\"literal:\"):\n67\t        return \"literal:\" + sha256_hex(src[len(\"literal:\"):].encode(\"utf-8\"))[:16]\n68\t    return src\n69\t\n70\t\n71\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n72\tclass WebStep:\n73\t    service: str\n74\t    kind: str                                    # navigate|fill|submit|account.create|login|purchase\n75\t    url: str\n76\t    fields: dict = field(default_factory=dict)   # {form_field: \"vault:password\"|\"vault:email\"|\"vault:username\"|\"literal:...\"}\n77\t    # NOTE: there is deliberately NO identity/impersonate field — fields resolve ONLY from the owner's own vault.\n78\t\n79\t\n80\tclass Delegate(Agent):\n81\t    name = \"DELEGATE\"\n82\t    mandate = \"owner-consented identity/account manager + web actor; per-action approved, offense-free\"\n83\t    ceiling = Tier.A2\n84\t\n85\t    def __init__(self, store=None, *, scope: ActorScope, vault=None, engine=None,\n86\t                 classifier=None, trusted_pubkey=None):\n87\t        super().__init__(store)\n88\t        self.scope = scope\n89\t        self._vault = vault\n90\t        self.engine = engine or HttpEngine()\n91\t        self._classifier = classifier\n92\t        self._trusted = trusted_pubkey\n93\t\n94\t    def _vaultobj(self):\n95\t        if self._vault is None:\n96\t            from .vault import CredentialVault\n97\t            self._vault = CredentialVault()\n98\t        return self._vault\n99\t\n100\t    def _cls(self):\n101\t        if self._classifier is None:\n102\t            from .kernel_classify import KernelClassifier\n103\t            self._classifier = KernelClassifier()\n104\t        return self._classifier\n105\t\n106\t    def _tp(self):\n107\t        if self._trusted is None:\n108\t            from ..governor.identity import owner_pubkey\n109\t            self._trusted = owner_pubkey()\n110\t        return self._trusted\n111\t\n112\t    def _field_binding(self, service: str, fields: dict) -> str:\n113\t        \"\"\"A value-free binding: field NAMES + vault-references + literal-CONTENT-HASHES, prefixed with\n114\t        the current vault VERSION when any field references the vault (so a rotation → new token →\n115\t        re-approval). Deterministically reproducible at execute from the journaled fields + vault state.\"\"\"\n116\t        parts, uses_vault = [], False\n117\t        for k in sorted(fields):\n118\t            src = fields[k]\n119\t            if src.startswith(\"vault:\"):\n120\t                uses_vault = True\n121\t            parts.append(f\"{k}={_redact_source(src)}\")\n122\t        binding = \";\".join(parts)\n123\t        if uses_vault:\n124\t            rec = self._vaultobj().get_record(service)\n125\t            binding = f\"vaultv{rec.version if rec else 0}|{binding}\"\n126\t        return binding\n127\t\n128\t    # --- preview: dry-run GET, block-detect, journal off-spine, queue a per-action A3 (redacted) ---\n129\t    def preview(self, steps: List[WebStep]) -> tuple:\n130\t        res = AgentResult(agent=self.name)\n131\t        queued: List[int] = []\n132\t        for step in steps:\n133\t            if not self.scope.origin_allowed(step.url):\n134\t                self.store.append(kind=\"refusal\", source=\"agent\", actor=self.name,\n135\t                                  payload={\"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"refused\", \"requested\": step.url,\n136\t                                           \"reason\": \"target origin is not in the owner's ActorScope\"})\n137\t                res.notes.append(f\"REFUSED {step.url}: out of ActorScope\")\n138\t                continue\n139\t            if step.kind == \"account.create\" and not self.scope.creation_allowed(self.store, step.service):\n140\t                self.store.append(kind=\"refusal\", source=\"agent\", actor=self.name,\n141\t                                  payload={\"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"refused\", \"service\": step.service,\n142\t                                           \"reason\": \"per-service creation cap (mass account creation is out of doctrine)\"})\n143\t                res.notes.append(f\"REFUSED account.create {step.service}: creation cap\")\n144\t                continue\n145\t            bad = sorted(k for k, v in step.fields.items() if not _valid_source(v))\n146\t            if bad:                                          # fail-closed: no silently-dropped fields\n147\t                self.store.append(kind=\"refusal\", source=\"agent\", actor=self.name,\n148\t                                  payload={\"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"refused\", \"requested\": step.url,\n149\t                                           \"reason\": f\"unknown/invalid field source(s) for: {bad}\"})\n150\t                res.notes.append(f\"REFUSED {step.url}: invalid field source(s) {bad}\")\n151\t                continue\n152\t            pv = self.engine.fetch(step.url)                 # A0 dry-run GET — no mutation\n153\t            block = detect_block(pv.status, pv.html)\n154\t            if block:                                        # STOP + surface — never defeat a block\n155\t                self.store.append(kind=\"finding\", source=\"agent\", actor=self.name,\n156\t                                  payload={\"signal\": \"web.actor.blocked\", \"decision\": \"surfaced\", \"url\": step.url,\n157\t                                           \"reason\": block, \"tier\": \"A0\",\n158\t                                           \"summary\": f\"{step.url} is blocked ({block}) — surfaced, NOT defeated\"})\n159\t                res.notes.append(f\"BLOCKED {step.url}: {block} — surfaced, not defeated (doctrine)\")\n160\t                continue\n161\t            page_hash = sha256_hex(pv.html.encode(\"utf-8\", \"ignore\"))\n162\t            fb = self._field_binding(step.service, step.fields)\n163\t            token = action_token(step.service, step.kind, step.url, page_hash, fb)\n164\t            _secure(_ACTOR_HOME)\n165\t            _secure(_JOURNAL)\n166\t            # executable step (WITH any literal values) → 0700 journal, keyed by token; NEVER the spine\n167\t            (_JOURNAL / f\"{token}.json\").write_text(json.dumps(\n168\t                {\"service\": step.service, \"kind\": step.kind, \"url\": step.url, \"fields\": step.fields}),\n169\t                encoding=\"utf-8\")\n170\t            tier = self._cls().classify(_TOOL.get(step.kind, \"unknown.op\"))   # → A3 for the mutating kinds\n171\t            r = self._dispatch([Proposal(\"event\", {\n172\t                \"signal\": ACTION_SIGNAL, \"service\": step.service, \"step_kind\": step.kind, \"url\": step.url,\n173\t                \"page_sha256\": page_hash, \"field_binding\": fb, \"action_token\": token,\n174\t                \"journal\": str(_JOURNAL / f\"{token}.json\"),\n175\t                \"fields_redacted\": [{\"name\": k, \"source\": _redact_source(step.fields[k])}\n176\t                                    for k in sorted(step.fields)],   # NAMES + refs/hashes only — NO values\n177\t                \"subject\": f\"{step.kind} on {step.service} (awaiting owner approval)\"}, tier)])\n178\t            seq = r.queued[0][\"seq\"] if r.queued else (r.applied[0] if r.applied else None)\n179\t            if seq is not None:\n180\t                queued.append(seq)\n181\t        return queued, res\n182\t\n183\t    # --- execute: verified approval → re-fetch → block-before-act → re-derive token → last-instant act\n184\t    def execute(self, step_seq: int) -> AgentResult:\n185\t        res = AgentResult(agent=self.name)\n186\t        rec = self.store.get(step_seq)\n187\t        if rec is None or rec.payload.get(\"signal\") != ACTION_SIGNAL:\n188\t            res.notes.append(f\"seq {step_seq} is not a web-actor step\")\n189\t            return res\n190\t        token = rec.payload[\"action_token\"]\n191\t        if not action_approved(self.store, step_seq, token, self._tp()):\n192\t            res.notes.append(f\"step {step_seq} has no verified owner approval — NO request made\")\n193\t            return res\n194\t        # SINGLE-SHOT: one approval authorises exactly ONE action. A step that already produced an applied\n195\t        # record is refused (the append-only approval never expires, so without this guard one signature\n196\t        # would authorise unbounded repeats). Mirrors the Operator's single-shot undo (BLOCK-6).\n197\t        for r in self.store.iter_records(since_seq=step_seq):\n198\t            if (r.payload.get(\"signal\") == ACTION_SIGNAL and r.payload.get(\"status\") == \"applied\"\n199\t                    and r.payload.get(\"target_seq\") == step_seq):\n200\t                res.notes.append(f\"step {step_seq} was already executed (seq {r.seq}) — refusing a replay (per-action)\")\n201\t                return res\n202\t        try:                                                 # executable step lives OFF the spine (0700)\n203\t            j = json.loads(Path(rec.payload[\"journal\"]).read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n204\t            service, kind, url, fields = j[\"service\"], j[\"kind\"], j[\"url\"], j[\"fields\"]\n205\t        except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):\n206\t            res.notes.append(\"step journal missing/unreadable — cannot execute\")\n207\t            return res\n208\t        if not self.scope.origin_allowed(url):               # re-check scope at execute (belt-and-suspenders)\n209\t            self.store.append(kind=\"refusal\", source=\"agent\", actor=self.name,\n210\t                              payload={\"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"refused\", \"target_seq\": step_seq, \"requested\": url,\n211\t                                       \"reason\": \"the step URL is not in the owner's ActorScope at execute — aborted\"})\n212\t            res.notes.append(f\"ABORTED: {url} left the ActorScope — no action\")\n213\t            return res\n214\t        pv = self.engine.fetch(url)                          # re-fetch (anti-TOCTOU + block-before-act)\n215\t        block = detect_block(pv.status, pv.html)\n216\t        if block:\n217\t            self.store.append(kind=\"finding\", source=\"agent\", actor=self.name,\n218\t                              payload={\"signal\": \"web.actor.blocked\", \"decision\": \"surfaced\", \"url\": url,\n219\t                                       \"reason\": block, \"tier\": \"A0\",\n220\t                                       \"summary\": f\"{url} blocked at execute ({block}) — surfaced, no action\"})\n221\t            res.notes.append(f\"BLOCKED at execute {url}: {block} — surfaced, no action\")\n222\t            return res\n223\t        # RE-DERIVE the token from the CURRENT url + page hash + vault version + journaled fields; a changed\n224\t        # URL OR page OR a credential rotation OR a journal edit → mismatch → abort (never trust a field).\n225\t        fresh = action_token(service, kind, url, sha256_hex(pv.html.encode(\"utf-8\", \"ignore\")),\n226\t                             self._field_binding(service, fields))\n227\t        if fresh != token:\n228\t            self.store.append(kind=\"refusal\", source=\"agent\", actor=self.name,\n229\t                              payload={\"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"refused\", \"target_seq\": step_seq,\n230\t                                       \"reason\": \"url/page/credential/step changed since approval (token mismatch) — aborted\"})\n231\t            res.notes.append(\"ABORTED: the world changed since approval (token mismatch) — no action\")\n232\t            return res\n233\t        # RE-CHECK the creation cap at execute (against committed applied records) so a batch previewed\n234\t        # before any execute cannot outrun cap=1 — the preview check alone counts zero applied.\n235\t        if kind == \"account.create\" and not self.scope.creation_allowed(self.store, service):\n236\t            self.store.append(kind=\"refusal\", source=\"agent\", actor=self.name,\n237\t                              payload={\"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"refused\", \"target_seq\": step_seq, \"service\": service,\n238\t                                       \"reason\": \"per-service creation cap reached at execute (mass creation out of doctrine)\"})\n239\t            res.notes.append(f\"REFUSED account.create {service} at execute: creation cap\")\n240\t            return res\n241\t        resolved = self._resolve(service, fields)            # credentials → locals ONLY (never the spine)\n242\t        out = self.engine.act({\"url\": url, \"kind\": kind}, resolved,\n243\t                              pinned_ip=getattr(pv, \"resolved_ip\", \"\") or None)   # bind the POST to the vetted IP\n244\t        seq = self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"agent\", actor=self.name,\n245\t                                payload={\"signal\": ACTION_SIGNAL, \"status\": \"applied\", \"target_seq\": step_seq,\n246\t                                         \"service\": service, \"step_kind\": kind, \"url\": url, \"action_token\": token,\n247\t                                         \"result_ok\": bool(out.get(\"ok\")), \"tier\": \"A1\", \"decision\": \"auto\",\n248\t                                         \"summary\": f\"{kind} on {service} executed (ok={out.get('ok')})\"})\n249\t        res.applied.append(seq)\n250\t        res.notes.append(f\"executed {kind} on {service} (ok={out.get('ok')})\")\n251\t        return res\n252\t\n253\t    def _resolve(self, service: str, fields: dict) -> dict:\n254\t        \"\"\"Resolve field references to VALUES at the last instant — into a local dict, never the spine.\"\"\"\n255\t        out: dict = {}\n256\t        rec = self._vaultobj().get_record(service)\n257\t        for name, src in fields.items():\n258\t            if src == \"vault:password\":\n259\t                out[name] = self._vaultobj().resolve_password(service) or \"\"\n260\t            elif src == \"vault:email\":\n261\t                out[name] = rec.email if rec else \"\"\n262\t            elif src == \"vault:username\":\n263\t                out[name] = rec.username if rec else \"\"\n264\t            elif src.startswith(\"literal:\"):\n265\t                out[name] = src[len(\"literal:\"):]\n266\t        return out\n267\t"}]},"uuid":"c29b41bd-ca5a-4dbb-9b77-7ca6897457c5","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:29:23.054Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"c208f2b7-7484-46be-9107-f4a69eaba7b8","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"4eb1047e-9571-4d4e-86e0-1831b951d5c1","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"5ef833af-9b6b-4bc3-a5b8-ed99e00a9fe6","agentId":"a9f88e85e8b6db51d","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_017tPWTKnyZFxGEkWq8Vr5qk","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Action gate (Phase 8, WS-G G-iii) — a near-verbatim mirror of `perception/egress.py`, but for web\n2\tACTIONS. `action_token = sha256(service|step|url|page_sha256|field_binding)` binds an approval to ONE\n3\texact action — the destination URL is IN the preimage (so an approval can never be rebound to a\n4\tdifferent URL, even one serving identical bytes), alongside the page hash (a page-change aborts) and\n5\tthe field binding (a vault-version bump / literal edit aborts). `action_approved` requires a VERIFIED\n6\towner/authorized-device approval whose signed `target_seq` IS that step — a replay onto a different\n7\taction, a wrong token, an unsigned/denied approval → False (fail-closed).\"\"\"\n8\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n9\t\n10\tfrom ..reuse import sha256_hex\n11\tfrom .approvals import SIGNAL as _APPROVAL_SIGNAL\n12\tfrom .approvals import verify_approval\n13\t\n14\tACTION_SIGNAL = \"web.actor.step\"\n15\t\n16\t\n17\tdef action_token(service: str, step_kind: str, url: str, page_sha256: str, field_binding: str) -> str:\n18\t    return sha256_hex(f\"{service}|{step_kind}|{url}|{page_sha256}|{field_binding}\".encode(\"utf-8\"))\n19\t\n20\t\n21\tdef action_approved(store, seq: int, token: str, trusted_pubkey) -> bool:\n22\t    rec = store.get(seq)\n23\t    if rec is None or rec.payload.get(\"signal\") != ACTION_SIGNAL or rec.payload.get(\"action_token\") != token:\n24\t        return False\n25\t    from ..mesh import authorized_devices\n26\t    devices = authorized_devices(store, trusted_pubkey)\n27\t    for r in store.iter_records(since_seq=seq):\n28\t        p = r.payload\n29\t        if (p.get(\"signal\") == _APPROVAL_SIGNAL and p.get(\"target_seq\") == seq\n30\t                and p.get(\"approval\") == \"approved\" and verify_approval(r, trusted_pubkey, extra_pubkeys=devices)):\n31\t            return True\n32\t    return False\n33\t"}]},"uuid":"6170d245-2659-4837-b7a2-5de9933b1ff0","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:29:32.390Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"4eb1047e-9571-4d4e-86e0-1831b951d5c1","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"6170d245-2659-4837-b7a2-5de9933b1ff0","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a9f88e85e8b6db51d","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdAK7rx52fA777PaBgoyG","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01DMwqTKZTNkqhttUK8K3kZ9","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/agents/actor_scope.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":6410,"cache_read_input_tokens":42533,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":6410,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdAK7qprS7vW86GQZyLvL","attributionAgent":"red-pen","type":"assistant","uuid":"c0047445-10e3-420f-bdc2-f198095ecdaa","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:29:32.880Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"c0047445-10e3-420f-bdc2-f198095ecdaa","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"5ef833af-9b6b-4bc3-a5b8-ed99e00a9fe6","agentId":"a9f88e85e8b6db51d","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01DMwqTKZTNkqhttUK8K3kZ9","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"ActorScope (Phase 8, WS-G G-iv) — the origin allowlist that bounds where DELEGATE may act (the\n2\towner's OWN service integrations), mirroring `OperatorScope`: EMPTY = deny-all. A step's origin must\n3\tbe (1) http/https, (2) public (`sources.is_public_host` — no internal hosts), (3) in the allowlist.\n4\tPlus a per-service `creation_cap` (default 1) — mass account creation is out of doctrine; the cap is\n5\tchecked at preview AND execute.\"\"\"\n6\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n7\t\n8\tfrom typing import List, Optional\n9\tfrom urllib.parse import urlsplit\n10\t\n11\tfrom .sources import is_public_host\n12\t\n13\t_DEFAULT_PORT = {\"http\": 80, \"https\": 443}\n14\t\n15\t\n16\tdef _origin(url: str) -> Optional[str]:\n17\t    \"\"\"Canonical scheme://host:port (default port made explicit) — so `https://h` and `https://h:443`\n18\t    match, but `https://h:8443` does NOT match `https://h` (port confusion is refused).\"\"\"\n19\t    try:\n20\t        p = urlsplit(url if \"//\" in url else \"https://\" + url)\n21\t        if p.scheme not in (\"http\", \"https\") or not p.hostname:\n22\t            return None\n23\t        port = p.port or _DEFAULT_PORT[p.scheme]\n24\t    except ValueError:                      # malformed / out-of-range port → fail-closed\n25\t        return None\n26\t    return f\"{p.scheme}://{p.hostname.lower()}:{port}\"\n27\t\n28\t\n29\tclass ActorScope:\n30\t    def __init__(self, allowed_origins: Optional[List[str]] = None, *, creation_cap: int = 1):\n31\t        self.allowed = {o for o in (_origin(a) for a in (allowed_origins or [])) if o}\n32\t        self.creation_cap = creation_cap\n33\t\n34\t    def origin_allowed(self, url: str) -> bool:\n35\t        p = urlsplit(url)\n36\t        if p.scheme not in (\"http\", \"https\") or not p.hostname or not is_public_host(p.hostname):\n37\t            return False\n38\t        return bool(self.allowed) and _origin(url) in self.allowed\n39\t\n40\t    def creation_allowed(self, store, service: str) -> bool:\n41\t        n = sum(1 for r in store.iter_records()\n42\t                if r.payload.get(\"signal\") == \"web.actor.step\"\n43\t                and r.payload.get(\"step_kind\") == \"account.create\"\n44\t                and r.payload.get(\"service\") == service\n45\t                and r.payload.get(\"status\") == \"applied\")\n46\t        return n < self.creation_cap\n47\t"}]},"uuid":"ef7d86ab-1ef0-4385-9b9f-6a624c5f3eb3","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:29:32.897Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"c0047445-10e3-420f-bdc2-f198095ecdaa","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"ef7d86ab-1ef0-4385-9b9f-6a624c5f3eb3","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a9f88e85e8b6db51d","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdAK7rx52fA777PaBgoyG","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01PfVdsLVnRGTYB8eFwJpjgU","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/agents/web_engine.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":6410,"cache_read_input_tokens":42533,"output_tokens":297,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":6410},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":297,"cache_read_input_tokens":42533,"cache_creation_input_tokens":6410,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":6410,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdAK7qprS7vW86GQZyLvL","attributionAgent":"red-pen","type":"assistant","uuid":"2810f5bf-7add-46fa-92ed-de6257383e2a","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:29:33.171Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"2810f5bf-7add-46fa-92ed-de6257383e2a","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"5ef833af-9b6b-4bc3-a5b8-ed99e00a9fe6","agentId":"a9f88e85e8b6db51d","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01PfVdsLVnRGTYB8eFwJpjgU","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Web engine (Phase 8, WS-G G-iii) — HTTP-only. `HttpEngine` reuses the SSRF-gated, IP-pinned,\n2\tcorrelatable-UA `sources.fetch_raw` for the read path and pins the SAME vetted IP for the write path\n3\t(so the bytes that were block-checked/hashed and the endpoint that receives credentials are one\n4\taddress — no DNS-rebinding split). `detect_block` is a BEST-EFFORT flag for a CAPTCHA / 403 / 429 /\n5\tCF-challenge; a DETECTED block STOPS + is surfaced as a positive control (it is not a guarantee that\n6\tevery anti-automation page is recognised — a missed soft-block only ever means the actor proceeds as\n7\tapproved, never that a block is defeated). There is deliberately NO headless-browser / JS-render path\n8\tin this actor: \"use a browser to beat a block\" is unreachable because the capability does not exist.\n9\t`FakeEngine` is the deterministic double + call spy for tests.\"\"\"\n10\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n11\t\n12\timport re\n13\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass\n14\tfrom typing import Optional, Protocol, runtime_checkable\n15\t\n16\t_BLOCK = re.compile(r\"recaptcha|hcaptcha|turnstile|cf-chl|just a moment|attention required|\\bcaptcha\\b\", re.I)\n17\t\n18\t\n19\t@dataclass\n20\tclass PageView:\n21\t    ok: bool\n22\t    status: int\n23\t    html: str\n24\t    url: str\n25\t    resolved_ip: str = \"\"    # the vetted IP the read used — threaded into act() to bind the write to it\n26\t\n27\t\n28\t@runtime_checkable\n29\tclass WebEngine(Protocol):\n30\t    egresses: bool\n31\t    def fetch(self, url: str) -> PageView: ...\n32\t    def act(self, step: dict, resolved: dict, *, pinned_ip: Optional[str] = None) -> dict: ...\n33\t\n34\t\n35\tdef detect_block(status: int, html: str) -> Optional[str]:\n36\t    \"\"\"A best-effort block signal (STOP + surface as a positive control — never defeated). Runs BEFORE\n37\t    any action. Keys on 403/429 and a fixed anti-automation keyword set; it can MISS a soft-block.\"\"\"\n38\t    if status in (403, 429):\n39\t        return f\"http-{status}\"\n40\t    if _BLOCK.search(html or \"\"):\n41\t        return \"captcha/anti-automation\"\n42\t    return None\n43\t\n44\t\n45\tclass HttpEngine:\n46\t    egresses = True\n47\t\n48\t    def fetch(self, url: str) -> PageView:\n49\t        from .sources import fetch_raw\n50\t        r = fetch_raw(url)\n51\t        return PageView(r.ok, r.status, r.raw, url, resolved_ip=r.resolved_ip)\n52\t\n53\t    def act(self, step: dict, resolved: dict, *, pinned_ip: Optional[str] = None) -> dict:\n54\t        \"\"\"A simple static-form submit (urlencoded POST). Credentials arrive in `resolved` at the last\n55\t        instant and go ONLY into the POST body — never the URL, never logged/returned. The socket is\n56\t        pinned to `pinned_ip` when the caller supplies the IP its read already vetted (binding the write\n57\t        to the block-checked address); otherwise the IP is re-vetted here (fail-closed on private).\"\"\"\n58\t        import urllib.parse\n59\t        import urllib.request\n60\t        from urllib.parse import urlsplit\n61\t        from .sources import UA, _NoRedirect, _PinnedHTTPHandler, _PinnedHTTPSHandler, _vetted_ip\n62\t        url = step.get(\"url\", \"\")\n63\t        ip = pinned_ip or _vetted_ip(urlsplit(url).hostname or \"\")\n64\t        if ip is None:\n65\t            return {\"ok\": False, \"reason\": \"ssrf-refused\"}\n66\t        data = urllib.parse.urlencode(resolved).encode(\"utf-8\")\n67\t        opener = urllib.request.build_opener(urllib.request.ProxyHandler({}), _PinnedHTTPHandler(ip),\n68\t                                             _PinnedHTTPSHandler(ip), _NoRedirect)\n69\t        req = urllib.request.Request(url, data=data, method=\"POST\",\n70\t                                     headers={\"User-Agent\": UA, \"Content-Type\": \"application/x-www-form-urlencoded\"})\n71\t        try:\n72\t            with opener.open(req, timeout=20) as r:\n73\t                return {\"ok\": 200 <= getattr(r, \"status\", 200) < 400, \"status\": getattr(r, \"status\", 200)}\n74\t        except Exception as e:  # noqa: BLE001\n75\t            return {\"ok\": False, \"reason\": f\"{type(e).__name__}\"}   # type name only — never the body/exception text\n76\t\n77\t\n78\tclass FakeEngine:\n79\t    \"\"\"Deterministic double + spy: `pages` = {url: (status, html)}; records every fetch/act (act records\n80\t    the field KEYS and the pinned IP only — never a value).\"\"\"\n81\t    egresses = True\n82\t\n83\t    def __init__(self, pages: Optional[dict] = None, *, resolved_ip: str = \"203.0.113.7\"):\n84\t        self.pages = pages or {}\n85\t        self.resolved_ip = resolved_ip\n86\t        self.calls: list = []\n87\t\n88\t    def fetch(self, url: str) -> PageView:\n89\t        self.calls.append((\"fetch\", url))\n90\t        st, html = self.pages.get(url, (404, \"\"))\n91\t        return PageView(200 <= st < 300, st, html, url, resolved_ip=self.resolved_ip)\n92\t\n93\t    def act(self, step: dict, resolved: dict, *, pinned_ip: Optional[str] = None) -> dict:\n94\t        self.calls.append((\"act\", step.get(\"url\"), sorted(resolved.keys()), pinned_ip))   # keys + IP only — never values\n95\t        return {\"ok\": True, \"status\": 200}\n96\t"}]},"uuid":"b83d76e1-86d2-437f-b958-6c693e2207f6","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:29:33.189Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"2810f5bf-7add-46fa-92ed-de6257383e2a","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"44c29e62-ced3-40a1-ad58-27aaf105af45","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"5ef833af-9b6b-4bc3-a5b8-ed99e00a9fe6","agentId":"a9f88e85e8b6db51d","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01A99HvmHoECgqZZ5hZmYWLf","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"CredentialVault (Phase 8, WS-G G-i) — the owner's OWN per-service credentials. Extends\n2\t`platform.secrets.SecretStore` preserving its invariant verbatim (\"Secrets NEVER enter the append-only\n3\tspine, a log, or a network payload\"). A `VaultRecord` has NO `password` field — only a `password_ref`\n4\t(a keyring key name); the password lives in the OS keyring. The manifest (email/username/ref/version)\n5\tlives in a 0700 dir OFF the append-only spine. The password is resolved from the keyring ONLY at\n6\texecute time, into a local variable — never assigned to a Proposal payload, never logged, never\n7\tjournaled. `version` bumps on every edit via `set_record` and BINDS an approval (rotate through the\n8\tvault API → version bump → re-approval), so the spine binds by `service+vault_ref+version` —\n9\tdeliberately NOT a hash of the value (hashing a low-entropy identity field onto an append-only log is\n10\titself a weak-preimage leak). NOTE: a password rotated OUT-OF-BAND directly in the keyring under the\n11\tsame `password_ref` (bypassing `set_record`) does not bump `version`; owner credential rotation should\n12\tgo through `set_record` so the version binding stays meaningful.\"\"\"\n13\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n14\t\n15\timport json\n16\timport os\n17\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\n18\tfrom typing import List, Optional\n19\t\n20\tfrom ..config import SIGIL_HOME\n21\t\n22\t_VAULT = SIGIL_HOME / \"vault\"\n23\t_MANIFEST = _VAULT / \"manifest.json\"\n24\t\n25\t\n26\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n27\tclass VaultRecord:\n28\t    service: str\n29\t    email: str = \"\"\n30\t    username: str = \"\"\n31\t    password_ref: str = \"\"            # keyring key name — NOT the password\n32\t    notes: str = \"\"\n33\t    version: int = 1\n34\t\n35\t\n36\tclass CredentialVault:\n37\t    def __init__(self, secret_store=None):\n38\t        from ..platform.secrets import SecretStore\n39\t        self.secrets = secret_store or SecretStore()\n40\t\n41\t    def _load(self) -> dict:\n42\t        try:\n43\t            data = json.loads(_MANIFEST.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\n44\t        except (OSError, ValueError):\n45\t            return {}\n46\t        return data if isinstance(data, dict) else {}      # a hostile/corrupt non-dict manifest → empty, not a crash\n47\t\n48\t    def _save(self, data: dict) -> None:\n49\t        _VAULT.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n50\t        try:\n51\t            os.chmod(_VAULT, 0o700)\n52\t        except OSError:\n53\t            pass\n54\t        fd = os.open(str(_MANIFEST), os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_TRUNC, 0o600)  # 0600 up-front\n55\t        with os.fdopen(fd, \"w\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n56\t            json.dump(data, f)\n57\t        try:\n58\t            os.chmod(str(_MANIFEST), 0o600)                 # enforce 0600 even if the file pre-existed with looser perms\n59\t        except OSError:\n60\t            pass\n61\t\n62\t    def set_record(self, service: str, *, email: str = \"\", username: str = \"\",\n63\t                   password: Optional[str] = None, notes: str = \"\") -> VaultRecord:\n64\t        ref = f\"vault/{service}/password\"\n65\t        if password is not None:\n66\t            self.secrets.set(ref, password)          # → keyring (or 0600 sigil.env), never the spine\n67\t        data = self._load()\n68\t        prev = data.get(service, {})\n69\t        rec = {\"service\": service, \"email\": email or prev.get(\"email\", \"\"),\n70\t               \"username\": username or prev.get(\"username\", \"\"), \"password_ref\": ref,\n71\t               \"notes\": notes or prev.get(\"notes\", \"\"), \"version\": int(prev.get(\"version\", 0)) + 1}\n72\t        data[service] = rec\n73\t        self._save(data)\n74\t        return VaultRecord(**rec)\n75\t\n76\t    @staticmethod\n77\t    def _rec(d) -> Optional[VaultRecord]:\n78\t        if not isinstance(d, dict):\n79\t            return None\n80\t        try:\n81\t            return VaultRecord(**d)                         # tolerate extra/missing keys in a hostile manifest\n82\t        except TypeError:\n83\t            return None\n84\t\n85\t    def get_record(self, service: str) -> Optional[VaultRecord]:\n86\t        return self._rec(self._load().get(service))\n87\t\n88\t    def records(self) -> List[VaultRecord]:\n89\t        return [r for r in (self._rec(d) for d in self._load().values()) if r is not None]\n90\t\n91\t    def resolve_password(self, service: str) -> Optional[str]:\n92\t        \"\"\"Fetch the password from the keyring — call ONLY at execute-time, into a local var.\"\"\"\n93\t        rec = self.get_record(service)\n94\t        return self.secrets.get(rec.password_ref) if rec else None\n95\t"}]},"uuid":"7450908f-ca1b-4fa6-833c-415116b46d10","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:29:37.799Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"44c29e62-ced3-40a1-ad58-27aaf105af45","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

A\n9\trefused/unreadable source is simply empty evidence, never a fabricated one.\"\"\"\n10\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n11\t\n12\timport http.client\n13\timport ipaddress\n14\timport re\n15\timport socket\n16\timport urllib.error\n17\timport urllib.request\n18\tfrom dataclasses import dataclass, field\n19\tfrom pathlib import Path\n20\tfrom typing import Optional\n21\tfrom urllib.parse import urlsplit\n22\t\n23\t# One stable, CORRELATABLE User-Agent (OBSIDIAN OPSEC §VI: identifiable, NOT evasive — never rotated).\n24\tUA = \"SIGIL-SCHOLAR/1.0 (authorized owner research)\"\n25\t\n26\t_TAG = re.compile(r\"<[^>]+>\")\n27\t_WS = re.compile(r\"\\s+\")\n28\t_SCRIPT = re.compile(r\"<(script|style)[^>]*>.*?</\\1>\", re.S | re.I)\n29\t\n30\t\n31\tdef _strip_html(html: str) -> str:\n32\t    html = _SCRIPT.sub(\" \", html)\n33\t    return _WS.sub(\" \", _TAG.sub(\" \", html)).strip()\n34\t\n35\t\n36\tdef _vetted_ip(host: str) -> Optional[str]:\n37\t    \"\"\"Resolve `host`; return ONE address to pin iff EVERY resolved address is public unicast, else\n38\t    None. Fail-closed: an unresolvable host, or any private/loopback/link-local/reserved/multicast/\n39\t    unspecified/IPv4-mapped-internal address, yields None.\"\"\"\n40\t    if not host:\n41\t        return None\n42\t    try:\n43\t        infos = socket.getaddrinfo(host, None)\n44\t    except (socket.gaierror, UnicodeError, OSError):\n45\t        return None\n46\t    if not infos:\n47\t        return None\n48\t    chosen = None\n49\t    for family, _t, _p, _c, sockaddr in infos:\n50\t        try:\n51\t            ip = ipaddress.ip_address(sockaddr[0])\n52\t        except ValueError:\n53\t            return None\n54\t        # unwrap IPv4-mapped/compat IPv6 (::ffff:127.0.0.1) so an internal v4 can't hide as v6\n55\t        if isinstance(ip, ipaddress.IPv6Address) and ip.ipv4_mapped is not None:\n56\t            ip = ip.ipv4_mapped\n57\t        # Reject anything NOT globally routable — `not is_global` also closes RFC 6598 CGNAT/shared\n58\t        # space 100.64.0.0/10 (incl. the Alibaba metadata IP 100.100.100.200) and other non-global\n59\t        # ranges the explicit list misses (red-pen BLOCK-1). The explicit checks stay as defense-in-depth.\n60\t        if (ip.is_private or ip.is_loopback or ip.is_link_local or ip.is_reserved\n61\t                or ip.is_multicast or ip.is_unspecified or not ip.is_global):\n62\t            return None\n63\t        if chosen is None:\n64\t            chosen = sockaddr[0]\n65\t    return chosen\n66\t\n67\t\n68\tdef is_public_host(host: str) -> bool:\n69\t    return _vetted_ip(host) is not None\n70\t\n71\t\n72\tclass _PinnedHTTPConnection(http.client.HTTPConnection):\n73\t    pinned_ip: Optional[str] = None\n74\t\n75\t    def connect(self):\n76\t        self.sock = socket.create_connection((self.pinned_ip or self.host, self.port),\n77\t                                              self.timeout, self.source_address)\n78\t        if getattr(self, \"_tunnel_host\", None):\n79\t            self._tunnel()\n80\t\n81\t\n82\tclass _PinnedHTTPSConnection(http.client.HTTPSConnection):\n83\t    pinned_ip: Optional[str] = None\n84\t\n85\t    def connect(self):\n86\t        sock = socket.create_connection((self.pinned_ip or self.host, self.port),\n87\t                                        self.timeout, self.source_address)\n88\t        if getattr(self, \"_tunnel_host\", None):\n89\t            self.sock = sock\n90\t            self._tunnel()\n91\t            sock = self.sock\n92\t        # SNI + certificate validation still use the ORIGINAL hostname, not the pinned IP.\n93\t        self.sock = self._context.wrap_socket(sock, server_hostname=self.host)\n94\t\n95\t\n96\tclass _PinnedHTTPHandler(urllib.request.HTTPHandler):\n97\t    def __init__(self, ip):\n98\t        super().__init__()\n99\t        self._ip = ip\n100\t\n101\t    def http_open(self, req):\n102\t        ip = self._ip\n103\t\n104\t        def factory(host, **kw):\n105\t            c = _PinnedHTTPConnection(host, **kw)\n106\t            c.pinned_ip = ip\n107\t            return c\n108\t        return self.do_open(factory, req)\n109\t\n110\t\n111\tclass _PinnedHTTPSHandler(urllib.request.HTTPSHandler):\n112\t    def __init__(self, ip):\n113\t        super().__init__()\n114\t        self._ip = ip\n115\t\n116\t    def https_open(self, req):\n117\t        ip = self._ip\n118\t\n119\t        def factory(host, **kw):\n120\t            c = _PinnedHTTPSConnection(host, **kw)\n121\t            c.pinned_ip = ip\n122\t            return c\n123\t        return self.do_open(factory, req, context=self._context)\n124\t\n125\t\n126\tclass _NoRedirect(urllib.request.HTTPRedirectHandler):\n127\t    \"\"\"Refuse to follow 3xx — a public URL must not redirect SCHOLAR onto an internal host.\"\"\"\n128\t    def redirect_request(self, req, fp, code, msg, headers, newurl):\n129\t        return None\n130\t\n131\t\n132\t@dataclass(frozen=True)\n133\tclass FetchResult:\n134\t    \"\"\"The outcome of a raw SSRF-gated fetch. `raw` preserves newlines (robots/extraction need line\n135\t    structure); `status` is surfaced (unlike `read_source`, which swallows to '') so a caller can do\n136\t    robots/backoff on 429/503. `status == 0` = network error / SSRF-refused (nothing left the box).\"\"\"\n137\t    ok: bool\n138\t    status: int\n139\t    raw: str\n140\t    url: str\n141\t    headers: dict = field(default_factory=dict)\n142\t    reason: str = \"\"\n143\t    resolved_ip: str = \"\"    # the vetted, PINNED IP this fetch used (so a caller can bind a follow-up POST to the SAME address)\n144\t\n145\t\n146\tdef fetch_raw(ref: str, *, timeout: int = 20, max_bytes: int = 2_000_000) -> FetchResult:\n147\t    \"\"\"SSRF-gated raw GET (Phase 8 shared): resolve-once, verify-all-public, PIN the vetted IP, refuse\n148\t    redirects, correlatable UA. Surfaces the HTTP status and preserves the raw body. Never raises. A\n149\t    non-public/unresolvable host → ok=False, status=0 (no request leaves the machine).\"\"\"\n150\t    if not ref.startswith((\"http://\", \"https://\")):\n151\t        return FetchResult(False, 0, \"\", ref, reason=\"not-http\")\n152\t    host = urlsplit(ref).hostname or \"\"\n153\t    ip = _vetted_ip(host)\n154\t    if ip is None:\n155\t        return FetchResult(False, 0, \"\", ref, reason=\"ssrf-refused\")\n156\t    # ProxyHandler({}) disables env http_proxy/https_proxy so routing can't be diverted (N3); the\n157\t    # pinned handlers force the socket to the vetted IP regardless.\n158\t    opener = urllib.request.build_opener(urllib.request.ProxyHandler({}),\n159\t                                         _PinnedHTTPHandler(ip), _PinnedHTTPSHandler(ip), _NoRedirect)\n160\t    req = urllib.request.Request(ref, headers={\"User-Agent\": UA})\n161\t    try:\n162\t        with opener.open(req, timeout=timeout) as r:\n163\t            raw = r.read(max_bytes).decode(\"utf-8\", \"ignore\")\n164\t            return FetchResult(True, getattr(r, \"status\", 200), raw, ref, headers=dict(r.headers), resolved_ip=ip)\n165\t    except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:                 # 3xx-not-followed / 4xx / 5xx — surface the code\n166\t        body = \"\"\n167\t        try:\n168\t            body = e.read(max_bytes).decode(\"utf-8\", \"ignore\")\n169\t        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001\n170\t            pass\n171\t        return FetchResult(False, e.code, body, ref, headers=dict(e.headers or {}), reason=f\"http-{e.code}\", resolved_ip=ip)\n172\t    except (urllib.error.URLError, OSError, ValueError) as e:\n173\t        return FetchResult(False, 0, \"\", ref, reason=f\"neterr:{type(e).__name__}\")\n174\t\n175\t\n176\tdef read_source(ref: str, *, timeout: int = 20, max_chars: int = 20000) -> str:\n177\t    \"\"\"Return the text of a source. `http(s)://…` is SSRF-gated (via `fetch_raw`) then HTML-stripped;\n178\t    anything else is a local path. Never raises — returns '' on failure or refusal.\"\"\"\n179\t    if ref.startswith((\"http://\", \"https://\")):\n180\t        res = fetch_raw(ref, timeout=timeout)\n181\t        return _strip_html(res.raw)[:max_chars] if res.ok else \"\"\n182\t    try:\n183\t        return Path(ref).read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\", errors=\"ignore\")[:max_chars]\n184\t    except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — an unreadable source is simply empty evidence\n185\t        return \"\"\n186\t"}]},"uuid":"948a4a4e-c7af-4c7d-b0bc-cf307b79a9bd","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:29:38.146Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"b7695f8a-8275-4c0f-8a6f-64f088cf1c50","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"573252ac-e33d-4bb9-9b26-bdeb33cfabb0","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a9f88e85e8b6db51d","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdAK9AKcoocTDWP3BQvRW","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_014KZHzf9R578xX1yBWzDWG8","name":"Read","input":{"file_path":"/home/kali/sigil/sigil/spine/store.py"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":8753,"cache_read_input_tokens":58726,"output_tokens":234,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":8753},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":2,"output_tokens":234,"cache_read_input_tokens":58726,"cache_creation_input_tokens":8753,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":8753,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdAK98isegqSo8rVDATTB","attributionAgent":"red-pen","type":"assistant","uuid":"dda52610-db5e-4eea-882b-e84ea85d4f1a","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:29:50.293Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"573252ac-e33d-4bb9-9b26-bdeb33cfabb0","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"5ef833af-9b6b-4bc3-a5b8-ed99e00a9fe6","agentId":"a9f88e85e8b6db51d","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01F3rJD8VGFdzV5NGXa7WeCE","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Approval queue (SIGIL §5, §9.3 mobile bridge) — the human gate for A2/A3 proposals. An agent\n2\tQUEUES a proposal (governor → QUEUE); the owner APPROVES or DENIES it here. Authentication is an\n3\tEd25519 signature over the canonical (target, decision, approver) by the OWNER key.\n4\t\n5\tRED-PEN HARDENING (Phase 6):\n6\t • The trusted pubkey is the PERSISTED owner identity — NOT the key supplied to the queue, so an\n7\t   attacker cannot self-certify by bringing their own key (RP-APPROVAL-2). approve/deny require the\n8\t   supplied signing key to BE the trusted owner key.\n9\t • `pending()` treats a queued item as resolved ONLY when a superseding approval VERIFIES against the\n10\t   trusted owner key, and keys resolution off the SIGNED target_seq (not the raw supersedes_id) — so\n11\t   an unsigned/forged approval can't silently drop an item (RP-1/RP-APPROVAL-1), and a genuine\n12\t   approval of one item can't be replayed onto another (RP-APPROVAL-4). `verify_approval` is thereby\n13\t   on the enforcement path (RP-APPROVAL-3).\n14\t\n15\tThis records the DECISION; it executes no external effect (SIGIL has no send/deploy path). The\n16\ttransport (Telegram/WhatsApp over WireGuard, §9.3) is a seam over this authenticated core.\"\"\"\n17\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n18\t\n19\tfrom typing import List, Optional\n20\t\n21\tfrom ..reuse import canonical_json, sha256_hex, sign, verify_one\n22\tfrom ..spine.store import SpineStore\n23\t\n24\tSIGNAL = \"governor.approval\"\n25\t_QUEUED_STATUS = \"awaiting-approval\"\n26\t\n27\t\n28\tdef _approval_message(target_seq, decision: str, approver: str) -> bytes:\n29\t    m = canonical_json({\"target\": target_seq, \"decision\": decision, \"approver\": approver})\n30\t    return m if isinstance(m, bytes) else m.encode()\n31\t\n32\t\n33\tdef verify_approval(record, trusted_pubkey_b64: Optional[str], *, extra_pubkeys=None) -> bool:\n34\t    \"\"\"True iff the approval carries a valid signature over its (target, decision, approver) by the\n35\t    OWNER key OR by a currently-authorized DEVICE key (`extra_pubkeys`). The claimed `pubkey` must be\n36\t    in the allowed set — which is composed ONLY of owner-minted keys (the owner identity + the\n37\t    owner-signed device-authorization ledger, see `sigil.mesh`) — so a key the requester merely\n38\t    presents is never trusted (RP-APPROVAL-2). The signed `target_seq` still binds (RP-APPROVAL-4).\n39\t    Unsigned / not-in-allowed-set / no-trusted-key → False (fail-closed).\"\"\"\n40\t    p = record.payload if hasattr(record, \"payload\") else record\n41\t    sig, pub = p.get(\"sig\"), p.get(\"pubkey\")\n42\t    allowed = set()\n43\t    if trusted_pubkey_b64:\n44\t        allowed.add(trusted_pubkey_b64)\n45\t    if extra_pubkeys:\n46\t        allowed.update(extra_pubkeys)\n47\t    if not sig or not pub or pub not in allowed:\n48\t        return False\n49\t    msg = _approval_message(p.get(\"target_seq\"), p.get(\"approval\"), p.get(\"approver\"))\n50\t    return verify_one(pub, msg, sig)                # verify against the (owner-minted) claimed key\n51\t\n52\t\n53\tdef pending(store: SpineStore, trusted_pubkey_b64: Optional[str] = None, *, extra_pubkeys=None) -> List:\n54\t    \"\"\"Queued proposals with NO valid approval yet, oldest first. Resolution requires a VERIFIED\n55\t    owner/authorized-device approval; an unsigned/forged/unauthorized approval leaves the item\n56\t    pending (fail-closed).\"\"\"\n57\t    if trusted_pubkey_b64 is None:\n58\t        from ..governor.identity import owner_pubkey\n59\t        trusted_pubkey_b64 = owner_pubkey()\n60\t    resolved = set()\n61\t    queued = {}\n62\t    for r in store.iter_records():\n63\t        p = r.payload\n64\t        if p.get(\"signal\") == SIGNAL and verify_approval(r, trusted_pubkey_b64, extra_pubkeys=extra_pubkeys):\n65\t            resolved.add(p.get(\"target_seq\"))       # honor the SIGNED target, not raw supersedes_id\n66\t        if p.get(\"decision\") == \"queued\" and p.get(\"status\") == _QUEUED_STATUS:\n67\t            queued[r.seq] = r\n68\t    return [r for seq, r in sorted(queued.items()) if seq not in resolved]\n69\t\n70\t\n71\tclass ApprovalError(Exception):\n72\t    pass\n73\t\n74\t\n75\tclass ApprovalQueue:\n76\t    def __init__(self, store: Optional[SpineStore] = None, *, owner_key=None,\n77\t                 trusted_pubkey_b64: Optional[str] = None):\n78\t        from ..governor.identity import owner_keypair, owner_pubkey\n79\t        self.store = store or SpineStore()\n80\t        self.owner_key = owner_key if owner_key is not None else owner_keypair()\n81\t        # PINNED to the persisted owner identity — never defaulted to the supplied key (RP-APPROVAL-2)\n82\t        self.trusted_pubkey_b64 = trusted_pubkey_b64 if trusted_pubkey_b64 is not None else owner_pubkey()\n83\t\n84\t    def _target(self, seq: int):\n85\t        for r in pending(self.store, self.trusted_pubkey_b64):\n86\t            if r.seq == seq:\n87\t                return r\n88\t        raise ApprovalError(f\"seq {seq} is not a pending approval (already decided, or not queued)\")\n89\t\n90\t    def _decide(self, seq: int, decision: str, *, approver: str, reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n91\t        target = self._target(seq)\n92\t        # the signing key MUST be the trusted owner key — not merely \"some key present\" (RP-APPROVAL-2).\n93\t        if (self.owner_key is None or self.trusted_pubkey_b64 is None\n94\t                or self.owner_key.public_key_b64 != self.trusted_pubkey_b64):\n95\t            raise ApprovalError(\"approval requires the OWNER's signing key (missing or not the trusted key)\")\n96\t        msg = _approval_message(seq, decision, approver)\n97\t        sig = sign(self.owner_key.private_key_b64, msg)\n98\t        payload = {\"signal\": SIGNAL, \"approval\": decision, \"target_seq\": seq, \"target_kind\": target.kind,\n99\t                   \"target_tier\": target.payload.get(\"tier\"), \"approver\": approver, \"reason\": reason,\n100\t                   \"pubkey\": self.trusted_pubkey_b64, \"sig\": sig, \"msg_digest\": sha256_hex(msg),\n101\t                   \"tier\": \"A0\", \"decision\": \"auto\"}\n102\t        return self.store.append(kind=\"event\", source=\"governor\", actor=\"OWNER\",\n103\t                                 payload=payload, supersedes_id=seq)\n104\t\n105\t    def approve(self, seq: int, *, approver: str = \"owner\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n106\t        return self._decide(seq, \"approved\", approver=approver, reason=reason)\n107\t\n108\t    def deny(self, seq: int, *, approver: str = \"owner\", reason: str = \"\") -> int:\n109\t        return self._decide(seq, \"denied\", approver=approver, reason=reason)\n110\t"}]},"uuid":"cae0fced-7089-4084-bb25-fc36b54d9c30","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:29:50.300Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"573252ac-e33d-4bb9-9b26-bdeb33cfabb0","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}
{"parentUuid":"dda52610-db5e-4eea-882b-e84ea85d4f1a","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"5ef833af-9b6b-4bc3-a5b8-ed99e00a9fe6","agentId":"a9f88e85e8b6db51d","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_014KZHzf9R578xX1yBWzDWG8","type":"tool_result","content":"1\t\"\"\"Append-only, hash-chained JSONL spine (SIGIL §6.1, D1).\n2\t\n3\tReuses CRUCIBLE's tamper-evident chain verbatim (`sigil.reuse`): each line carries\n4\t`{seq, prev_hash, entry_hash}` where `entry_hash` links prev+cert_digest+seq. The\n5\t`cert_digest` is over the record's CONTENT only (scope/kind/source/actor/payload/\n6\tparent/supersedes) — NOT the wallclock `ts` — so the chain is replay-stable. Appends\n7\tare O(1): read the last line's entry, `append_entry`, write.\n8\t\"\"\"\n9\tfrom __future__ import annotations\n10\t\n11\timport json\n12\timport os\n13\tfrom pathlib import Path\n14\tfrom typing import Any, Iterator\n15\t\n16\tfrom ..config import SCOPE, SPINE_PATH\n17\tfrom ..reuse import ChainEntry, append_entry, build_chain, digest_payload, verify_chain\n18\tfrom .models import SpineRecord, now_iso\n19\t\n20\t\n21\tdef _last_nonempty_line(path: Path) -> str | None:\n22\t    \"\"\"Read the last non-empty line without loading the file (seek-from-end).\"\"\"\n23\t    with path.open(\"rb\") as f:\n24\t        f.seek(0, os.SEEK_END)\n25\t        end = f.tell()\n26\t        if end == 0:\n27\t            return None\n28\t        buf = b\"\"\n29\t        pos = end\n30\t        while pos > 0:\n31\t            step = min(8192, pos)\n32\t            pos -= step\n33\t            f.seek(pos)\n34\t            buf = f.read(step) + buf\n35\t            lines = [ln for ln in buf.split(b\"\\n\") if ln.strip()]\n36\t            if lines and (pos == 0 or buf.count(b\"\\n\") >= 2):\n37\t                return lines[-1].decode(\"utf-8\")\n38\t        lines = [ln for ln in buf.split(b\"\\n\") if ln.strip()]\n39\t        return lines[-1].decode(\"utf-8\") if lines else None\n40\t\n41\t\n42\tclass SpineStore:\n43\t    def __init__(self, path: Path | str = SPINE_PATH) -> None:\n44\t        self.path = Path(path)\n45\t        self.path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\n46\t        self._last: ChainEntry | None = self._read_last_entry()\n47\t\n48\t    # --- write --------------------------------------------------------------------\n49\t    def append(\n50\t        self, *, kind: str, source: str, actor: str, payload: dict[str, Any],\n51\t        parent_id: int | None = None, supersedes_id: int | None = None,\n52\t        ts: str | None = None,\n53\t    ) -> int:\n54\t        content = {\n55\t            \"scope\": SCOPE, \"kind\": kind, \"source\": source, \"actor\": actor,\n56\t            \"payload\": payload, \"parent_id\": parent_id, \"supersedes_id\": supersedes_id,\n57\t        }\n58\t        cert_digest = digest_payload(content)  # wallclock-free\n59\t        entry = append_entry([self._last], cert_digest) if self._last else build_chain([cert_digest])[0]\n60\t        record = {\n61\t            \"seq\": entry.seq, **content, \"ts\": ts or now_iso(),\n62\t            \"cert_digest\": cert_digest, \"prev_hash\": entry.prev_hash, \"entry_hash\": entry.entry_hash,\n63\t        }\n64\t        with self.path.open(\"a\", encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n65\t            f.write(json.dumps(record, ensure_ascii=False) + \"\\n\")\n66\t        self._last = entry\n67\t        return entry.seq\n68\t\n69\t    # --- read ---------------------------------------------------------------------\n70\t    def iter_records(self, *, since_seq: int = -1) -> Iterator[SpineRecord]:\n71\t        if not self.path.exists():\n72\t            return\n73\t        with self.path.open(encoding=\"utf-8\") as f:\n74\t            for line in f:\n75\t                line = line.strip()\n76\t                if not line:\n77\t                    continue\n78\t                d = json.loads(line)\n79\t                if d[\"seq\"] > since_seq:\n80\t                    yield SpineRecord.from_dict(d)\n81\t\n82\t    def get(self, seq: int) -> SpineRecord | None:\n83\t        for r in self.iter_records(since_seq=seq - 1):\n84\t            if r.seq == seq:\n85\t                return r\n86\t            if r.seq > seq:\n87\t                break\n88\t        return None\n89\t\n90\t    @property\n91\t    def next_seq(self) -> int:\n92\t        return (self._last.seq + 1) if self._last else 0\n93\t\n94\t    def count(self) -> int:\n95\t        return sum(1 for _ in self.iter_records())\n96\t\n97\t    # --- integrity ----------------------------------------------------------------\n98\t    def entries(self) -> list[ChainEntry]:\n99\t        return [\n100\t            ChainEntry(seq=r.seq, prev_hash=r.prev_hash, cert_digest=r.cert_digest, entry_hash=r.entry_hash)\n101\t            for r in self.iter_records()\n102\t        ]\n103\t\n104\t    def verify(self) -> tuple[bool, str]:\n105\t        \"\"\"Two-layer UNKEYED integrity: (1) BINDING — each record's payload still hashes to its\n106\t        stored cert_digest (catches silent payload edits); (2) CHAIN — the entries link cleanly\n107\t        (catches delete/reorder/entry tamper). This proves internal CONSISTENCY, not authenticity:\n108\t        a naive payload edit fails (1), and a mid-chain digest edit cascades an entry_hash/prev_hash\n109\t        break caught by (2) — BUT a writer who recomputes cert_digest+entry_hash for the tip (no\n110\t        successor to cascade into) or forward-cascades a fork produces a self-consistent chain that\n111\t        passes here. Resistance to a recompute-capable writer is the owner-SIGNED head's job\n112\t        (`checkpoint.verify_checkpoint`, Ed25519 + monotonic last_seq). Use this for corruption/\n113\t        naive-tamper detection; use the signed head for tamper-EVIDENCE.\"\"\"\n114\t        entries: list[ChainEntry] = []\n115\t        for r in self.iter_records():\n116\t            content = {\n117\t                \"scope\": r.scope, \"kind\": r.kind, \"source\": r.source, \"actor\": r.actor,\n118\t                \"payload\": r.payload, \"parent_id\": r.parent_id, \"supersedes_id\": r.supersedes_id,\n119\t            }\n120\t            if digest_payload(content) != r.cert_digest:\n121\t                return False, f\"binding break at seq {r.seq}: payload does not match cert_digest (record tampered)\"\n122\t            entries.append(ChainEntry(seq=r.seq, prev_hash=r.prev_hash, cert_digest=r.cert_digest, entry_hash=r.entry_hash))\n123\t        return verify_chain(entries)\n124\t\n125\t    def _read_last_entry(self) -> ChainEntry | None:\n126\t        line = _last_nonempty_line(self.path) if self.path.exists() else None\n127\t        if not line:\n128\t            return None\n129\t        d = json.loads(line)\n130\t        return ChainEntry(seq=d[\"seq\"], prev_hash=d[\"prev_hash\"], cert_digest=d[\"cert_digest\"], entry_hash=d[\"entry_hash\"])\n131\t"}]},"uuid":"166217b1-705e-4334-9840-91e6916291c4","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:29:50.316Z","sourceToolAssistantUUID":"dda52610-db5e-4eea-882b-e84ea85d4f1a","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

v=FakeVault(); v.add(\"svc\",username=\"owner\",email=\"me@o.ex\",password=\"PW\")\n    d=Delegate(store,scope=ActorScope(list(allowed),creation_cap=cap),vault=v,engine=e,classifier=FakeCls(),trusted_pubkey=OP)\n    return d,e\ndef _acts(e): return [c for c in e.calls if c[0]==\"act\"]\n\nprint(\"=== 3a: BLOCK-3 re-run: /login and /promo identical HTML, execute approved /login POSTs /login ===\")\ns=_store(); html=\"<form>identical bytes</form>\"\nd,e=_delegate(s,pages={f\"{SVC}/login\":(200,html),f\"{SVC}/promo\":(200,html)})\nq,_=d.preview([WebStep(\"svc\",\"login\",f\"{SVC}/login\",{\"pw\":\"vault:password\"}),\n               WebStep(\"svc\",\"login\",f\"{SVC}/promo\",{\"pw\":\"vault:password\"})])\na,b=q\nta,tb=s.get(a).payload[\"action_token\"],s.get(b).payload[\"action_token\"]\nja,jb=s.get(a).payload[\"journal\"],s.get(b).payload[\"journal\"]\nprint(f\"  tokens differ: {ta!=tb}  journals differ: {ja!=jb}\")\nApprovalQueue(s,owner_key=OWNER,trusted_pubkey_b64=OP).approve(a)\nr=d.execute(a)\nprint(f\"  execute approved /login -> applied={bool(r.applied)} POST url={_acts(e)[-1][1] if _acts(e) else None}\")\nassert ta!=tb and ja!=jb and _acts(e)[-1][1]==f\"{SVC}/login\", \"BLOCK-3 rebind\"\nprint(\"  3a HELD: url bound, no rebind\\n\")\n\nprint(\"=== 3b: origin re-check at execute runs BEFORE fetch/act (journal url edited to out-of-scope) ===\")\ns=_store()\nd,e=_delegate(s,pages={f\"{SVC}/login\":(200,html),\"https://evil.example/x\":(200,html)})\nq,_=d.preview([WebStep(\"svc\",\"login\",f\"{SVC}/login\",{\"pw\":\"vault:password\"})])\nApprovalQueue(s,owner_key=OWNER,trusted_pubkey_b64=OP).approve(q[0])\n# tamper the journal: rewrite url to an OUT-OF-SCOPE origin\njpath=Path(s.get(q[0]).payload[\"journal\"])\nimport json; OP=OWNER.public_key_b64\n_TMP=Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix=\"rp4-\")); actor_mod._ACTOR_HOME=_TMP; actor_mod._JOURNAL=_TMP/\"steps\"\n_PRIVATE={\"127.0.0.1\",\"localhost\",\"10.0.0.5\",\"169.254.169.254\",\"::1\"}\nscope_mod.is_public_host=lambda h:h.lower() not in _PRIVATE\nSENTINEL=\"SENTINEL-PW-9f3a2b-UNIQUE-DO-NOT-LEAK\"; SVC=\"https://svc.example\"\nclass FakeCls:\n    def classify(self,t): return Tier.A0 if t==\"http.get\" else Tier.A3\nclass FakeVault:\n    def __init__(self): self._r={}; self._pw={}\n    def add(self,s,**k): self._r[s]=VaultRecord(service=s,username=k.get(\"username\",\"\"),email=k.get(\"email\",\"\"),password_ref=f\"vault/{s}/pw\",version=1); self._pw[s]=k.get(\"password\",\"\")\n    def get_record(self,s): return self._r.get(s)\n    def resolve_password(self,s): return self._pw.get(s)\ndef _vault():\n    v=FakeVault(); v.add(\"svc\",username=\"owner\",email=\"me@o.ex\",password=SENTINEL); return v\ndef _store(): return SpineStore(tempfile.mktemp(suffix=\".jsonl\"))\ndef _delegate(store,*,pages=None,allowed=(SVC,),cap=1,vault=None,engine=None):\n    e=engine or FakeEngine(pages if pages is not None else {f\"{SVC}/signup\":(200,\"<form>signup</form>\")})\n    d=Delegate(store,scope=ActorScope(list(allowed),creation_cap=cap),vault=vault or _vault(),engine=e,classifier=FakeCls(),trusted_pubkey=OP)\n    return d,e\ndef _acts(e): return [c for c in e.calls if c[0]==\"act\"]\n\nprint(\"=== 4a: actor threads pv.resolved_ip into act (happy path) ===\")\ns=_store(); d,e=_delegate(s)\nq,_=d.preview([WebStep(\"svc\",\"login\",f\"{SVC}/signup\",{\"pw\":\"vault:password\"})])\nApprovalQueue(s,owner_key=OWNER,trusted_pubkey_b64=OP).approve(q[0]); d.execute(q[0])\nprint(f\"  act pinned_ip = {_acts(e)[-1][3]!r} (== fetch's vetted IP)\")\nassert _acts(e)[-1][3]==\"203.0.113.7\"\n\nprint(\"=== 4b: neterr-then-neterr degenerate path: does act get reached with empty ip? ===\")\n# A stub engine that mimics a REAL neterr: ok=False,status=0,html='',resolved_ip='' on BOTH fetches.\nclass NetErrEngine:\n    egresses=True\n    def __init__(self): self.calls=[]\n    def fetch(self,url):\n        self.calls.append((\"fetch\",url)); return PageView(False,0,\"\",url,resolved_ip=\"\")\n    def act(self,step,resolved,*,pinned_ip=None):\n        self.calls.append((\"act\",step.get(\"url\"),sorted(resolved.keys()),pinned_ip)); return {\"ok\":True,\"status\":200}\ns=_store(); d,e=_delegate(s,engine=NetErrEngine())\nq,_=d.preview([WebStep(\"svc\",\"login\",f\"{SVC}/signup\",{\"pw\":\"vault:password\"})])\nprint(f\"  neterr page queued at preview? {len(q)==1}  (detect_block(0,'')={detect_block(0,'')})\")\nif q:\n    ApprovalQueue(s,owner_key=OWNER,trusted_pubkey_b64=OP).approve(q[0]); r=d.execute(q[0])\n    acts=_acts(e)\n    print(f\"  execute applied={bool(r.applied)} acts={len(acts)} pinned_ip={acts[-1][3] if acts else None!r}\")\n    print(\"  NOTE: on a total fetch failure the token still matches (empty==empty) and act IS reached\")\n    print(\"        with pinned_ip=None -> HttpEngine.act re-vets via _vetted_ip (still SSRF fail-closed).\")\nprint(\"  4b: real-byte fetches ALWAYS carry a non-empty resolved_ip;
