d,e=_delegate(s)\ncases={\"env:PATH\":\"env\", \"file:/etc/passwd\":\"file\", \"vault:unknownkey\":\"unknown vault key\",\n       123:\"non-string int\", None:\"None\", \"literal:vault:password\":\"literal-that-looks-like-vaultref\",\n       \"literal:\":\"empty literal\", \"literal:a;b|c=vaultv9|x\":\"literal with separators\",\n       \"vault:email\":\"valid vault:email\", \"vault:password\":\"valid vault:password\"}\nfor src,desc in cases.items():\n    q,res=d.preview([WebStep(\"svc\",\"login\",f\"{SVC}/signup\",{\"f\":src})])\n    refused = q==[] and any(\"invalid field source\" in n for n in res.notes)\n    print(f\"  {desc:38s} src={src!r:35s} -> {'REFUSED' if refused else 'ACCEPTED(queued)'}\")\n# verify the literal-that-looks-like-vaultref does NOT spoof a vault binding in the token/redaction\nfrom sigil.agents.actor import _redact_source, _valid_source\nprint(f\"  _redact_source('literal:vault:password') = {_redact_source('literal:vault:password')!r}  (hashed, not 'vault:password')\")\nprint(f\"  _redact_source('literal:a;b|c=vaultv9|x') = {_redact_source('literal:a;b|c=vaultv9|x')!r}  (raw separators gone)\")\n# resolve of the look-alike literal returns the literal TEXT, not the real password\nd2,_=_delegate(_store())\nout=d2._resolve(\"svc\",{\"f\":\"literal:vault:password\"})\nprint(f\"  _resolve literal:vault:password -> {out!r}  (POSTs the TEXT 'vault:password', NOT {SENTINEL[:10]}...)\")\nassert out[\"f\"]==\"vault:password\" and SENTINEL not in str(out)\nprint(\"  5 HELD: unknown/non-string refused; {"parentUuid":"8a750673-77eb-48be-9684-44b0810681d1","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a9f88e85e8b6db51d","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdAKpDJJKAixKB7CAQQR6","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01RHZGMEVdUozFVRubVfxoeE","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"cat > /tmp/claude-1000/-home-kali-Pictures-PENTEST-main/7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27/scratchpad/rp_fuzz.py <<'PY'\n\"\"\"RED-PEN: regression fuzz on the NEW port-binding scope + field-name injection into field_binding.\"\"\"\nimport sigil.agents.actor_scope as scope_mod\nscope_mod.is_public_host = lambda h: h.lower() not in {\"127.0.0.1\",\"localhost\",\"10.0.0.5\",\"169.254.169.254\",\"::1\"}\nfrom sigil.agents.actor_scope import ActorScope, _origin\nfrom sigil.agents.actor import Delegate, _redact_source\n\nprint(\"=== A: _origin / origin_allowed fuzz — malformed inputs must fail-closed, never crash ===\")\nsc = ActorScope([\"https://svc.example\"])\nfuzz = [\"\", \"https://\", \"https://svc.example:99999/x\", \"https://svc.example:-1/x\", \"http://svc.example:0/x\",\n        \"https://[::1/x\", \"https://svc.example:abc/x\", \"ftp://svc.example/x\", \"javascript:alert(1)\",\n        \"https://svc.example./x\", \"https://SVC.EXAMPLE/x\", \"//svc.example/x\", \"https://user:pass@svc.example/x\",\n        \"https://svc.example:443@evil.example/x\", \"https://svc.example\\t/x\"]\nfor u in fuzz:\n    try:\n        o = _origin(u); a = sc.origin_allowed(u)\n        print(f\"  {u!r:45s} _origin={o!r:35s} allowed={a}\")\n    except Exception as ex:\n        print(f\"  !! CRASH on {u!r}: {type(ex).__name__}: {ex}\")\n\nprint(\"\\n=== B: allowlist with explicit port + the credentials-in-userinfo confusion ===\")\nsc2 = ActorScope([\"https://svc.example:8443\"])\nfor u in [\"https://svc.example:8443/x\",\"https://svc.example/x\",\"https://svc.example:443/x\"]:\n    print(f\"  allow=[svc.example:8443]  {u!r:35s} -> {sc2.origin_allowed(u)}\")\n# the userinfo trick: does 'https://svc.example:443@evil.example' resolve to evil or svc?\nprint(f\"  userinfo-confusion 'https://svc.example:443@evil.example/x' allowed by [svc.example]? \"\n      f\"{sc.origin_allowed('https://svc.example:443@evil.example/x')}\")\n\nprint(\"\\n=== C: field-NAME injection into field_binding (names are raw; values are hashed) ===\")\nclass V:\n    def get_record(self,s):\n        from sigil.agents.vault import VaultRecord\n        return VaultRecord(service=s, version=1)\nd = Delegate(store=None, scope=sc, vault=V())\n# genuine two-field binding\nb1 = d._field_binding(\"svc\", {\"user\":\"vault:username\",\"pw\":\"vault:password\"})\n# attacker tries to forge the SAME binding string via an injected field NAME\nb2 = d._field_binding(\"svc\", {\"user=vault:username;pw\":\"vault:password\"})\nb3 = d._field_binding(\"svc\", {\"pw\":\"vault:password\",\"user\":\"vault:username\"})  # order-independence check\nprint(f\"  genuine   : {b1!r}\")\nprint(f\"  name-inject: {b2!r}\")\nprint(f\"  reordered : {b3!r}\")\nprint(f\"  name-injection forges genuine binding? {b1==b2}   (order-independent? {b1==b3})\")\n# can a literal value inject separators to spoof a vault ref token entry?\nb4 = d._field_binding(\"svc\", {\"f\":\"literal:pw=vault:password;g\"})\nprint(f\"  literal-with-separators binding: {b4!r}  (raw ';','|','=' from the literal are hashed away)\")\nassert b1 != b2, \"NOTE if True: field-NAME injection can forge a binding\"\nprint(\"  => field NAMES are raw (an injected name changes the STRING but the field NAME differs, so it is a\")\nprint(\"     DIFFERENT step the owner sees & approves; VALUES are hashed so a literal cannot inject separators)\")\nPY\ncd /home/kali/sigil && ~/.sigil/venv/bin/python /tmp/claude-1000/-home-kali-Pictures-PENTEST-main/7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27/scratchpad/rp_fuzz.py","description":"Fuzz new scope port logic and field-name injection"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":926,"cache_read_input_tokens":111167,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":926,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdAKpBiJBRhoKyVdBM3U1","attributionAgent":"red-pen","type":"assistant","uuid":"fd8f813e-1c01-48bb-ac2b-71effe390313","timestamp":"2026-07-18T22:39:45.533Z","effort":"xhigh","userType":"external","entrypoint":"claude-vscode","cwd":"/home/kali/Pictures/PENTEST-main","sessionId":"7758e121-f349-47d5-886b-6bb5a1d60e27","version":"2.1.214","gitBranch":"main","slug":"parsed-popping-lemur"}

{"parentUuid":"bbb769ac-001f-464b-9241-fac9508e213e","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a9f88e85e8b6db51d","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-8","id":"msg_011CdAL3xn4iygqJP9ZN1VU2","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01Gua3WobVyc3MtiSXZtLJvS","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"cd /home/kali/sigil && git stash list >/dev/null 2>&1; echo \"=== Confirm the 3 headline guards are load-bearing (neutralize -> exploit revives on THIS code) ===\"\n~/.sigil/venv/bin/python - <<'PY'\nimport tempfile, types\nfrom pathlib import Path\nimport sigil.agents.actor as actor_mod\nimport sigil.agents.actor_scope as scope_mod\nimport sigil.agents.actor_gate as gate_mod\nscope_mod.is_public_host=lambda h:h.lower() not in {\"127.0.0.1\",\"localhost\",\"::1\"}\nfrom sigil.agents.actor import Delegate, WebStep\nfrom sigil.agents.actor_scope import ActorScope\nfrom sigil.agents.approvals import ApprovalQueue\nfrom sigil.agents.base import Tier\nfrom sigil.agents.vault import VaultRecord\nfrom sigil.agents.web_engine import FakeEngine\nfrom sigil.reuse import generate_keypair\nfrom sigil.spine.store import SpineStore\nOWNER=generate_keypair(); OP=OWNER.public_key_b64\n_TMP=Path(tempfile.mkdtemp()); actor_mod._ACTOR_HOME=_TMP; actor_mod._JOURNAL=_TMP/\"steps\"\nSVC=\"https://svc.example\"\nclass FakeCls:\n    def classify(self,t): return Tier.A0 if t==\"http.get\" else Tier.A3\nclass V:\n    def get_record(self,s): return VaultRecord(service=s,username=\"o\",version=1)\n    def resolve_password(self,s): return \"pw\"\ndef mk(pages,cap=1):\n    s=SpineStore(tempfile.mktemp(suffix=\".jsonl\")); e=FakeEngine(pages)\n    d=Delegate(s,scope=ActorScope([SVC],creation_cap=cap),vault=V(),engine=e,classifier=FakeCls(),trusted_pubkey=OP)\n    return s,d,e\ndef acts(e): return [c for c in e.calls if c[0]==\"act\"]\n\n# (1) neutralize the single-shot guard by monkeypatching iter_records to hide applied records\ns,d,e=mk({f\"{SVC}/buy\":(200,\"<form>buy</form>\")})\nq,_=d.preview([WebStep(\"svc\",\"purchase\",f\"{SVC}/buy\",{\"pw\":\"vault:password\"})])\nApprovalQueue(s,owner_key=OWNER,trusted_pubkey_b64=OP).approve(q[0])\nreal_iter=s.iter_records\ndef blind_iter(*a,**k):   # simulate the guard being absent (no applied record ever seen in the scan window)\n    for r in real_iter(*a,**k):\n        if r.payload.get(\"status\")==\"applied\": continue\n        yield r\n# with guard intact:\nn_intact=sum(1 for _ in range(3) if d.execute(q[0]).applied)\nprint(f\"  single-shot INTACT: acts={len(acts(e))} (expect 1)\")\n# with guard blinded:\ns2,d2,e2=mk({f\"{SVC}/buy\":(200,\"<form>buy</form>\")})\nq2,_=d2.preview([WebStep(\"svc\",\"purchase\",f\"{SVC}/buy\",{\"pw\":\"vault:password\"})])\nApprovalQueue(s2,owner_key=OWNER,trusted_pubkey_b64=OP).approve(q2[0])\n_ri=s2.iter_records\ns2.iter_records=lambda *a,**k:(r for r in _ri(*a,**k) if r.payload.get(\"status\")!=\"applied\")\nfor _ in range(3): d2.execute(q2[0])\nprint(f\"  single-shot BLINDED: acts={len(acts(e2))} (revives to 3 -> guard is load-bearing)\")\n\n# (2) neutralize url-in-token by reverting action_token to the OLD (no-url) preimage\nfrom sigil.reuse import sha256_hex\nold_token=lambda service,kind,url,page,fb: sha256_hex(f\"{service}|{kind}|{page}|{fb}\".encode())\nhtml=\"<form>identical</form>\"\ns3,d3,e3=mk({f\"{SVC}/login\":(200,html),f\"{SVC}/promo\":(200,html)})\ngate_mod.action_token=old_token; Conclusion: one signature can never be replayed into repeats; a second act always costs a second owner signature. Pre-existing, not a fix regression, and every creation still requires a per-action owner A3 signature.\n\n**3. URL bound into token + journal keyed by token — HELD.** BLOCK-3 re-run: `/login` and `/promo` with byte-identical HTML now get **different tokens and different journals**; executing the approved `/login` POSTs to `/login`. The execute-time `origin_allowed` re-check runs **before** any fetch/act (a journal url tampered to `https://evil.example` aborts with **zero** extra fetches), and a journal url edited to a different in-scope url (identical HTML) aborts on token mismatch because the url is in the preimage. The fallback is sound: with `pinned_ip=None` **or** `\"\"`, `HttpEngine.act` re-vets via `_vetted_ip` and refuses a loopback (`ssrf-refused`). I traced the only path where `resolved_ip` is empty — a total network error (`neterr`, status 0, no bytes) — and confirmed every *byte-bearing* response (2xx, and even 4xx/5xx with a body) carries a non-empty pinned IP, so the rebind gap does **not** reopen for any real fetch. `tests/test_scrape.py` → 13/13 (the shared `fetch_raw` `resolved_ip` add did not break SCRIBE).\n\n**5. field-source validation — HELD.** `env:PATH`, `file:/etc/passwd`, `vault:unknownkey`, `123`, and `None` are all **REFUSED at preview** (no silent drop, no crash). Literals are accepted and reduced to a 16-char content hash in the binding (`_redact_source`), so a literal cannot inject the `;`/`|`/`=` separators; `literal:vault:password` resolves to the literal *text* `\"vault:password\"`, never the real secret, and cannot spoof a `vault:` binding entry (`literal:` ≠ `vault:` prefix). Field *names* are raw, but an injected name yields a *different* binding string → a different token → a different owner-visible step, not a cross-step forgery.\n\n**6. honesty — HELD.** `web_engine.py` and `actor.py` contain no `BrowserEngine`, `needs_js`, `headless`, `playwright`, `webdriver`, `selenium`, `subprocess`, or `escalate`. The surviving `needs_js` lives only in `sigil/scrape/render.py` (WS-E SCRIBE), and I confirmed importing the actor pulls in **no** render/browser module. Exactly one `.act(` call site. The real Rust WARDEN oracle classifies `account.create/login/submit.form/purchase.item/form.fill/browser.navigate` → A3 and `http.get` → A0, so \"every action is A3, no promotion\" is deterministically enforced, not merely asserted.\n\n**7. credential-leak re-scan (fixed code) — HELD.** Full preview→approve→execute plus every new refusal type (single-shot, invalid-source, cap) → the unique sentinel is **absent** from the spine bytes, the 0700 journal, and every recorded engine call.
